## ERROR CATALOG — CAT v2

Envelope `LocalizedException → {code, messageAr, messageEn}`; codes follow `{MOD}-{http}[-{SLUG}]`; statuses are those the platform emits. Messages are copied character for character from the SRS. The SRS gives no text for the PLATFORM-STD rows, so their ar / en text is written here; every row, RULE or PLATFORM-STD, has both messages and both bundle entries (checklist C11, ADR-CAT-044, ADR-CAT-065). Rows with a 2xx status are success notices carried in `ApiResponse<T>` (ADR-CAT-048).

```yaml name=error-catalog
rows:
  - {code: "CAT-422-NAME-AR-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-001, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-018, API-CAT-019, API-CAT-021, API-CAT-023, API-CAT-025, API-CAT-027, API-CAT-029, API-CAT-030], http: 422, trigger: "nameAr missing, longer than 200 characters or without an Arabic letter", messages: {ar: "أدخل الاسم بالعربية (حتى 200 حرف) متضمنًا حرفًا عربيًا واحدًا على الأقل.", en: "Enter the Arabic name (up to 200 characters) with at least one Arabic letter."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-NAME-EN-TOO-LONG", rule: RULE-CAT-002, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-018, API-CAT-019, API-CAT-021, API-CAT-023, API-CAT-025, API-CAT-027, API-CAT-029, API-CAT-030], http: 422, trigger: "nameEn longer than 200 characters", messages: {ar: "الاسم بالإنجليزية لا يتجاوز 200 حرف.", en: "The English name can have at most 200 characters."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-ITEM-NAME-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-003, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-005], http: 409, trigger: "another active Item of the tenant has the same normalised nameAr ({code} = that Item's code, from QR-CAT-002)", messages: {ar: "يوجد صنف نشط بالاسم نفسه: {code}. افتح الصنف الموجود أو غيّر الاسم.", en: "An active item already has this name: {code}. Open the existing item or change the name."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-CODE-SYSTEM-ASSIGNED", rule: RULE-CAT-004, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-008], http: 422, trigger: "a request supplies or changes the code of an Item or a Variant", messages: {ar: "يُعيَّن الرمز تلقائيًا ولا يمكن إدخاله أو تعديله.", en: "The code is assigned automatically and cannot be entered or changed."}}
  - {code: "CAT-500-CODE-NOT-ISSUED", rule: RULE-CAT-005, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-006, API-CAT-015, API-CAT-049], http: 500, trigger: "NumberSeriesApi returns no code for CAT_ITEM, CAT_VARIANT or CAT_INTERNAL_BARCODE; nothing is stored", messages: {ar: "تعذّر إصدار رمز جديد الآن، ولم يُحفظ شيء. أعد المحاولة بعد لحظات.", en: "A new code could not be issued, and nothing was saved. Try again in a moment."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-LOOKUP-VALUE-INACTIVE", rule: RULE-CAT-006, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-005, API-CAT-008, API-CAT-010, API-CAT-014, API-CAT-015, API-CAT-032, API-CAT-033, API-CAT-035, API-CAT-036], http: 422, trigger: "a changed lookup-backed field holds a code that is not an active value of its key", messages: {ar: "القيمة المختارة غير متاحة. اختر قيمة نشطة من القائمة.", en: "The selected value is not available. Choose an active value from the list."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-BASE-UOM-FIXED", rule: RULE-CAT-007, api: [API-CAT-003], http: 422, trigger: "an update changes baseUomCode of a saved Item", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن تغيير وحدة القياس الأساسية بعد حفظ الصنف. أضف وحدة أخرى للصنف بدلًا من ذلك.", en: "The base unit cannot be changed after the item is saved. Add another unit to the item instead."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-CAPABILITY-OFF", rule: RULE-CAT-008, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-006, API-CAT-017, API-CAT-025, API-CAT-027, API-CAT-029, API-CAT-030], http: 422, trigger: "creating or updating a Variant attribute or value, or generating Variants, while `variants` is off; creating a BUNDLE Item or saving Bundle components while `bundles` is off ({flag}); a read, deactivation or reactivation is never refused", messages: {ar: "هذه الوظيفة غير مفعّلة لنشاطك. فعّل مفتاح القدرة «{flag}» من الإعدادات لاستخدامها.", en: "This function is not switched on for your business. Turn on the «{flag}» capability flag in settings to use it."}}
  - {code: "CAT-500-CAPABILITY-UNREADABLE", rule: RULE-CAT-009, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-006, API-CAT-017, API-CAT-025, API-CAT-027, API-CAT-029, API-CAT-030], http: 500, trigger: "the FeatureFlags read returns no value for the flag an operation of RULE-CAT-008 depends on", messages: {ar: "تعذّر التحقق من إعدادات القدرات الآن، ولم يُحفظ شيء. أعد المحاولة بعد لحظات.", en: "Capability settings could not be checked, and nothing was saved. Try again in a moment."}}
  - {code: "CAT-200-VARIANTS-IN-USE", rule: RULE-CAT-010, api: [API-CAT-051], http: 200, trigger: "turn-off check of `variants` while active assignments exist on active Variants of active Items (allowed = false; {count})", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن إيقاف المتغيّرات لأن {count} صنفًا يستخدمها. أوقف متغيّرات هذه الأصناف أو أبقِ المتغيّرات مفعّلة.", en: "Variants cannot be turned off because {count} items use them. Deactivate the variants of these items or keep variants on."}}
  - {code: "CAT-200-BUNDLES-IN-USE", rule: RULE-CAT-011, api: [API-CAT-051], http: 200, trigger: "turn-off check of `bundles` while active BUNDLE Items exist (allowed = false; {count})", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن إيقاف الحزم لوجود {count} حزمة نشطة. أوقف هذه الحزم أو أبقِ الحزم مفعّلة.", en: "Bundles cannot be turned off because {count} active bundles exist. Deactivate these bundles or keep bundles on."}}
  - {code: "CAT-201-EXISTING-VARIANTS-KEPT", rule: RULE-CAT-012, api: [API-CAT-006], http: 201, trigger: "success notice: existing Variants took the first selected values and kept their codes and barcodes", messages: {ar: "احتفظت المتغيّرات الحالية بأرقامها وباركوداتها وأُسندت إليها القيم الأولى المختارة.", en: "Existing variants kept their codes and barcodes and took the first selected values."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-VARIANT-COMBINATION-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-013, api: [API-CAT-006], http: 409, trigger: "the requested combination is already carried by another active Variant of the Item", messages: {ar: "هذه التركيبة من القيم موجودة في متغيّر آخر لهذا الصنف.", en: "This combination of values already exists on another variant of this item."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-VARIANT-LIMIT-EXCEEDED", rule: RULE-CAT-014, api: [API-CAT-006], http: 422, trigger: "generation would give the Item more than 3 attributes or 300 active Variants", messages: {ar: "الحد الأقصى 3 خصائص و300 متغيّر نشط للصنف الواحد.", en: "An item can have at most 3 attributes and 300 active variants."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-LAST-ACTIVE-VARIANT", rule: RULE-CAT-015, api: [API-CAT-009], http: 409, trigger: "deactivating the last active Variant of an active Item", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن إيقاف آخر متغيّر نشط لصنف نشط. أوقف الصنف نفسه بدلًا من ذلك.", en: "The last active variant of an active item cannot be deactivated. Deactivate the item instead."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-ATTRIBUTE-CODE-NAME-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-016, api: [API-CAT-025, API-CAT-027, API-CAT-029, API-CAT-030, API-CAT-054, API-CAT-055], http: 409, trigger: "attribute / value code taken in its scope, nameAr taken among active records (also when the record is reactivated), or code not 1–30 Latin letters, digits or hyphens", messages: {ar: "الرمز أو الاسم مستخدم بالفعل. استخدم رمزًا (حتى 30 حرفًا لاتينيًا أو رقمًا أو شرطة) واسمًا مختلفين.", en: "The code or name is already used. Use a different code (up to 30 Latin letters, digits or hyphens) and name."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-ATTRIBUTE-VALUE-IN-USE", rule: RULE-CAT-017, api: [API-CAT-028, API-CAT-031], http: 409, trigger: "deactivating an attribute or value that an active assignment of an active Variant carries ({count})", messages: {ar: "هذه الخاصية أو القيمة مستخدمة في {count} متغيّرًا نشطًا. أوقف هذه المتغيّرات أولًا.", en: "This attribute or value is used by {count} active variants. Deactivate those variants first."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-BUNDLE-COMPONENTS-REQUIRED", rule: RULE-CAT-018, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-017], http: 422, trigger: "a BUNDLE saved with no active line, or a quantity not above zero / with more than 3 decimals", messages: {ar: "أضف مكوّنًا واحدًا على الأقل بكمية أكبر من صفر (حتى 3 أرقام عشرية).", en: "Add at least one component with a quantity above zero (up to 3 decimals)."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-BUNDLE-COMPONENT-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-019, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-005, API-CAT-017], http: 422, trigger: "at Bundle save or Bundle reactivation, a component Variant is inactive or of an inactive Item, belongs to a BUNDLE Item, or is listed twice", messages: {ar: "المكوّن يجب أن يكون متغيّرًا نشطًا لصنف ليس حزمة، ولا يتكرر في الحزمة نفسها.", en: "A component must be an active variant of an item that is not a bundle, listed once per bundle."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-BUNDLE-SINGLE-VARIANT", rule: RULE-CAT-020, api: [API-CAT-006], http: 422, trigger: "Variant generation or attribute assignment for an Item of kind BUNDLE", messages: {ar: "الحزمة لها متغيّر واحد فقط ولا تقبل خصائص المتغيّرات.", en: "A bundle has one variant only and takes no variant attributes."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-VARIANT-IN-ACTIVE-BUNDLE", rule: RULE-CAT-021, api: [API-CAT-004, API-CAT-009], http: 409, trigger: "deactivating a Variant, or its Item, while it is an active component of an active Bundle ({count})", messages: {ar: "هذا المتغيّر مكوّن في {count} حزمة نشطة. أزله من هذه الحزم أو أوقفها أولًا.", en: "This variant is a component of {count} active bundles. Remove it from those bundles or deactivate them first."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-ITEM-KIND-FIXED", rule: RULE-CAT-022, api: [API-CAT-003], http: 422, trigger: "an update changes itemKindCode of a saved Item", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن تغيير نوع الصنف بعد حفظه. أنشئ صنفًا جديدًا بالنوع المطلوب.", en: "The item kind cannot be changed after saving. Create a new item of the required kind."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-CATEGORY-PARENT-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-023, api: [API-CAT-021, API-CAT-023, API-CAT-053], http: 422, trigger: "parent inactive (also when the Category is reactivated), the Category itself or one of its sub-categories, or depth above 5", messages: {ar: "الفئة الأم يجب أن تكون نشطة، وألا تكون الفئة نفسها أو إحدى فئاتها الفرعية، وألا يتجاوز العمق 5 مستويات.", en: "The parent category must be active, must not be the category itself or one of its sub-categories, and the depth cannot exceed 5 levels."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-CATEGORY-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-024, api: [API-CAT-021, API-CAT-023, API-CAT-053], http: 409, trigger: "Category code taken, nameAr taken among active siblings (also when the Category is reactivated), or code not 1–30 Latin letters, digits or hyphens", messages: {ar: "يوجد رمز الفئة نفسه، أو الاسم نفسه تحت الفئة الأم نفسها. استخدم رمزًا أو اسمًا مختلفًا.", en: "The category code already exists, or the name already exists under the same parent. Use a different code or name."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-CATEGORY-IN-USE", rule: RULE-CAT-025, api: [API-CAT-024], http: 409, trigger: "deactivating a Category with active sub-categories or active Items ({count})", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن إيقاف الفئة لوجود {count} فئة فرعية أو صنف نشط فيها. انقلها أو أوقفها أولًا.", en: "The category cannot be deactivated while it holds {count} active sub-categories or items. Move or deactivate them first."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-CATEGORY-INACTIVE", rule: RULE-CAT-026, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-005], http: 422, trigger: "a changed categoryFk, or the categoryFk of an Item being reactivated, points to an inactive Category", messages: {ar: "الفئة المختارة غير نشطة. اختر فئة نشطة.", en: "The selected category is inactive. Choose an active category."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-ITEM-UNIT-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-027, api: [API-CAT-010], http: 422, trigger: "unit equals the base unit or another active unit of the Item, or factor not above zero / more than 6 decimals", messages: {ar: "الوحدة يجب أن تختلف عن الوحدة الأساسية وألا تتكرر، ومعامل التحويل أكبر من صفر (حتى 6 أرقام عشرية).", en: "The unit must differ from the base unit and appear once, with a conversion factor above zero (up to 6 decimals)."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-CONVERSION-FACTOR-FIXED", rule: RULE-CAT-028, api: [API-CAT-012], http: 422, trigger: "an update changes conversionFactor of a saved Item unit", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن تعديل معامل التحويل بعد الحفظ. أوقف هذه الوحدة وأضفها من جديد بالمعامل الصحيح.", en: "The conversion factor cannot be changed after saving. Deactivate this unit and add it again with the correct factor."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-BARCODE-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-029, api: [API-CAT-014, API-CAT-015], http: 409, trigger: "barcodeValue equals an active barcode of the tenant ({code} = owning Item code)", messages: {ar: "هذا الباركود مستخدم في الصنف {code}. افتح ذلك الصنف أو استخدم باركودًا آخر.", en: "This barcode is already used by item {code}. Open that item or use another barcode."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-GTIN-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-030, api: [API-CAT-014], http: 422, trigger: "GTIN not 8, 12, 13 or 14 digits, or wrong GS1 check digit", messages: {ar: "باركود GTIN يجب أن يتكون من 8 أو 12 أو 13 أو 14 رقمًا برقم تحقق صحيح.", en: "A GTIN barcode must have 8, 12, 13 or 14 digits with a valid check digit."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-BARCODE-UNIT-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-031, api: [API-CAT-014, API-CAT-015], http: 422, trigger: "barcode unit is neither the Item's base unit nor an active Item unit", messages: {ar: "وحدة الباركود يجب أن تكون الوحدة الأساسية للصنف أو إحدى وحداته النشطة.", en: "The barcode unit must be the item's base unit or one of its active units."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-BARCODE-SCALE-PREFIX", rule: RULE-CAT-032, api: [API-CAT-014, API-CAT-015, API-CAT-032], http: 409, trigger: "a GTIN / INTERNAL barcode begins with an active layout key, or a new layout key begins an active GTIN / INTERNAL barcode ({prefix})", messages: {ar: "الباركود يبدأ ببادئة ملصق ميزان نشط ({prefix}). استخدم باركودًا آخر أو عدّل إعداد الميزان.", en: "The barcode starts with the prefix of an active scale label layout ({prefix}). Use another barcode or change the scale settings."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-SCALE-CODE-LENGTH", rule: RULE-CAT-033, api: [API-CAT-014], http: 422, trigger: "SCALE barcode not all digits, of a length no active layout uses, or no active layout exists ({length})", messages: {ar: "رمز الميزان يجب أن يتكون من {length} أرقام، ويلزم وجود إعداد ملصق ميزان نشط.", en: "The scale code must have {length} digits, and an active scale label layout is required."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-SCALE-LAYOUT-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-034, api: [API-CAT-032, API-CAT-033], http: 422, trigger: "prefix outside 02 / 20–29 or used by another active layout, scaleCodeLength outside 4–6, valueDecimals outside 0–3", messages: {ar: "البادئة رقمان (02 أو من 20 إلى 29) غير مستخدمة في إعداد نشط آخر، وطول رمز الميزان من 4 إلى 6، والأرقام العشرية من 0 إلى 3.", en: "The prefix is two digits (02 or 20 to 29) not used by another active layout, the scale code length is 4 to 6, and the decimals are 0 to 3."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-SCALE-LABEL-UNREADABLE", rule: RULE-CAT-035, api: [API-CAT-043], http: 422, trigger: "a value with an active layout prefix has a wrong check digit or an unknown scale code", messages: {ar: "تعذّر قراءة ملصق الميزان: رقم التحقق غير صحيح أو رمز الميزان غير معرَّف. اطلب الصنف بالاسم أو أضف رمز الميزان إليه.", en: "The scale label could not be read: the check digit is wrong or the scale code is not defined. Find the item by name or add the scale code to it."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-LABEL-COPIES-OUT-OF-RANGE", rule: RULE-CAT-036, api: [API-CAT-050], http: 422, trigger: "fewer than 1 or more than 999 copies of a Variant, or more than 5,000 labels", messages: {ar: "عدد النسخ لكل متغيّر من 1 إلى 999، وإجمالي الملصقات لا يتجاوز 5000.", en: "Copies per variant are 1 to 999, and the total cannot exceed 5,000 labels."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-LABEL-VARIANT-NO-BARCODE", rule: RULE-CAT-037, api: [API-CAT-050], http: 422, trigger: "a label request lists Variants without an active barcode ({count}; the Variants are listed)", messages: {ar: "لا يوجد باركود نشط لـ {count} متغيّر. أنشئ لها باركودًا داخليًا ثم اطبع.", en: "{count} variants have no active barcode. Generate an internal barcode for them, then print."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-IMAGE-NOT-ACCEPTED", rule: RULE-CAT-038, api: [API-CAT-018], http: 422, trigger: "image not JPG / PNG / WEBP, above 5 MB, or a 21st active image", messages: {ar: "الصورة يجب أن تكون JPG أو PNG أو WEBP بحجم لا يتجاوز 5 ميجابايت، وبحد أقصى 20 صورة نشطة للصنف.", en: "The image must be JPG, PNG or WEBP of at most 5 MB, with at most 20 active images per item."}}
  - {code: "CAT-200-PRIMARY-IMAGE-REPLACED", rule: RULE-CAT-039, api: [API-CAT-019], http: 200, trigger: "success notice: the selected image became the main image in place of the former one", messages: {ar: "للصنف صورة رئيسية واحدة فقط؛ تم تعيين الصورة المختارة رئيسية بدل السابقة.", en: "An item has exactly one main image; the selected image replaced the previous main image."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-MEDIA-VARIANT-MISMATCH", rule: RULE-CAT-040, api: [API-CAT-018, API-CAT-019], http: 422, trigger: "variantFk of a media record belongs to another Item", messages: {ar: "المتغيّر المختار لا ينتمي إلى هذا الصنف.", en: "The selected variant does not belong to this item."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-PUBLISH-BLOCKED", rule: RULE-CAT-041, api: [API-CAT-005], http: 422, trigger: "publishing an inactive Item or an Item with no active Variant", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن نشر صنف غير نشط أو بلا متغيّر نشط. فعّل الصنف أو أحد متغيّراته أولًا.", en: "An inactive item, or one with no active variant, cannot be published. Activate the item or one of its variants first."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-EINVOICE-CODE-INVALID", rule: RULE-CAT-042, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-008], http: 422, trigger: "only one of type and code, a GS1 code not 8–14 digits, or an EGS code not starting with EG- / above 100 characters", messages: {ar: "أدخل نوع رمز الفاتورة الإلكترونية والرمز معًا: GS1 من 8 إلى 14 رقمًا، وEGS يبدأ بـ EG- ولا يتجاوز 100 حرف.", en: "Enter the e-invoice code type and code together: GS1 is 8 to 14 digits, EGS starts with EG- and has at most 100 characters."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-UNIT-EINVOICE-DUPLICATE", rule: RULE-CAT-043, api: [API-CAT-035], http: 409, trigger: "a Unit e-invoice code is created for a unit that already has an active one", messages: {ar: "لهذه الوحدة رمز فاتورة إلكترونية نشط بالفعل. عدّله بدلًا من إضافة رمز جديد.", en: "This unit already has an active e-invoice unit. Change it instead of adding a new one."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-IMPORT-FILE-REJECTED", rule: RULE-CAT-044, api: [API-CAT-049], http: 422, trigger: "import file not xlsx, above 10 MB, above 5,000 data rows, or with a different header row; no Item is created", messages: {ar: "الملف غير مقبول: يجب أن يكون بصيغة xlsx بحجم لا يتجاوز 10 ميجابايت وحتى 5000 صف، وبعناوين ورقة الاستيراد نفسها. نزّل ورقة الاستيراد واستخدمها.", en: "The file was not accepted: it must be xlsx, at most 10 MB and 5,000 rows, with the import sheet headers. Download the import sheet and use it."}}
  - {code: "CAT-200-IMPORT-ROW-REJECTED", rule: RULE-CAT-045, api: [API-CAT-049], http: 200, trigger: "per rejected row inside a completed import result ({row}, {column}, {reason} = the failed rule's message)", messages: {ar: "الصف {row}، العمود {column}: {reason}. صحّح الصف وأعد استيراده.", en: "Row {row}, column {column}: {reason}. Correct the row and import it again."}}
  - {code: "CAT-400-SEARCH-TERM-TOO-SHORT", rule: RULE-CAT-046, api: [API-CAT-047], http: 400, trigger: "catalogue search term shorter than 2 characters; no results", messages: {ar: "اكتب حرفين على الأقل للبحث.", en: "Type at least 2 characters to search."}}
  - {code: "CAT-404-BARCODE-NOT-FOUND", rule: RULE-CAT-047, api: [API-CAT-043], http: 404, trigger: "no active barcode of an active Variant of an active Item and no active scale label layout matches the looked-up value", messages: {ar: "لا يوجد صنف بهذا الباركود. ابحث بالاسم أو أضف الباركود إلى الصنف.", en: "No item has this barcode. Search by name or add the barcode to the item."}}
  - {code: "CAT-200-DEACTIVATED", rule: RULE-CAT-048, api: [API-CAT-004, API-CAT-009, API-CAT-013, API-CAT-016, API-CAT-024], http: 200, trigger: "success notice after a soft deactivation of a SHARED record", messages: {ar: "تم الإيقاف. يبقى السجل متاحًا للمستندات السابقة ولا يظهر في الاختيارات الجديدة.", en: "Deactivated. The record stays available to earlier documents and no longer appears in new selections."}}
  - {code: "CAT-500-CHANGE-NOT-SAVED", rule: RULE-CAT-049, api: [API-CAT-001, API-CAT-003, API-CAT-004, API-CAT-005, API-CAT-006, API-CAT-008, API-CAT-009, API-CAT-010, API-CAT-012, API-CAT-013, API-CAT-014, API-CAT-015, API-CAT-016, API-CAT-017, API-CAT-018, API-CAT-019, API-CAT-020, API-CAT-049], http: 500, trigger: "the commit of a catalogue change fails and the transaction rolls back; no event is delivered", messages: {ar: "تعذّر حفظ التغيير ولم يُرسل أي إشعار بالتغيير. أعد المحاولة.", en: "The change was not saved and no change notice was sent. Try again."}}
  - {code: "CAT-422-ITEM-NO-ACTIVE-VARIANT", rule: RULE-CAT-050, api: [API-CAT-005], http: 422, trigger: "reactivating an Item that has no active Variant", messages: {ar: "لا يمكن إعادة تفعيل صنف بلا متغيّر نشط. أنشئ متغيّرًا له أو أنشئ صنفًا جديدًا.", en: "An item with no active variant cannot be reactivated. Generate a variant for it, or create a new item."}}
  - {code: "CAT-409-ITEM-UNIT-HAS-BARCODES", rule: RULE-CAT-051, api: [API-CAT-013], http: 409, trigger: "deactivating an Item unit while active barcodes of the Item's Variants are bound to it ({count})", messages: {ar: "هذه الوحدة مستخدمة في {count} باركود نشط. أوقف هذه الباركودات أولًا.", en: "This unit is used by {count} active barcodes. Deactivate those barcodes first."}}
```

## API DOCUMENT
`api-spec-cat.yaml` (OpenAPI 3.1.0) is derived from the API blocks and this catalog in the same run: one operation per `API-*` block (path and method from its `Endpoint` line, `x-api-id`, `x-traces`), request parameters and bodies from its `Request` line, the success response from its `Response` line, and one response per catalog row the block's `Errors` line names, keyed by the row's status with the row's code under `x-error-codes`; bearer JWT security on every operation; paged operations carry `page` and `size`.

Contract coverage: CON-CAT-001 … CON-CAT-018 — 18 of 18 honoured (entity items also in the PHASE DATA-DOM entity blocks; the in-process interface `com.erp.cat.crossmodule.CatalogueReadApi` carries the eleven operation items).

### Query catalogue
QR-CAT-001 … 010 → ENT-CAT-001 · 011 … 017 → ENT-CAT-002 · 018 … 021 → ENT-CAT-005 · 022 … 026 → ENT-CAT-003 · 027 … 031 → ENT-CAT-004 · 032 … 038 → ENT-CAT-006 · 039 … 042 → ENT-CAT-007 · 043 … 046, 071 → ENT-CAT-008 · 047 … 053, 072 → ENT-CAT-009 · 054 … 058 → ENT-CAT-010 · 059 … 064 → ENT-CAT-011 · 065 … 070 → ENT-CAT-012. Every QR is reached by ≥ 1 API and every API with a database operation cites its QRs (API-CAT-048 and API-CAT-052 have none). Native / join / lock deviations: ADR-CAT-047.

### Entities / tables bound
12 of 12 entities bound to their db-script tables (CAT_ITEM, CAT_VARIANT, CAT_VAR_ATTR, CAT_VAR_ATTR_VALUE, CAT_VAR_ATTR_ASSIGN, CAT_CATEGORY, CAT_BUNDLE_COMP, CAT_ITEM_UNIT, CAT_BARCODE, CAT_ITEM_MEDIA, CAT_SCALE_LABEL_LAYOUT, CAT_UNIT_EINVOICE_CODE); 161 of 161 DBF in the DB Alignment Manifest; 0 PENDING DB. Lookups reused: the 10 CAT-owned keys of SRS A6 (CAT_ITEM_KIND, CAT_PUBLISH_STATE, CAT_BRAND, CAT_UOM, CAT_TAX_CLASS, CAT_EINVOICE_ITEM_CODE_TYPE, CAT_EINVOICE_UNIT, CAT_BARCODE_TYPE, CAT_SCALE_VALUE_TYPE, CAT_CHANGE_KIND); new keys: none.

### Integration
XM: none — the register is `records: []`; no edge minted; `INT-C` (the last phase) carries no block; no integration package.

### Catalog
60 codes: 53 RULE rows (one per RULE-CAT-001 … 053; 6 of them are 2xx success notices — RULE-CAT-010, 011, 012, 039, 045, 048) + 7 PLATFORM-STD rows (6 of ADR-CAT-044 — the 413 and 415 rows left with the multipart surface, ADR-CAT-056 — and CAT-404-LOOKUP-KEY-UNKNOWN of ADR-CAT-065). Every row carries its ar and en message, PLATFORM-STD rows included (checklist C11). Rules without a message: none. No RULE is DEFERRED.

### API document
api-spec-cat.yaml — OpenAPI 3.1.0, validated · operations 55 = API blocks 55 · paths 34 · catalog rows answered 60 = catalog rows 60 · no multipart request body (ADR-CAT-056) · bearer JWT security on every operation · paged operations (API-CAT-038, 040, 042, 046) carry page / size.

### Security
6 pages (CAT_ITEMS, CAT_CATEGORIES, CAT_VARIANT_ATTRIBUTES, CAT_ITEM_IMPORT, CAT_BARCODE_LABELS, CAT_SETTINGS) · 20 permission names `PERM_<PAGE_CODE>_<ACTION>` derived by the given `sec` module · preset-role grants per SRS B4 (ADR-CAT-046).

### ALIGN
Verdict as stamped by the orchestrator from `gov.py analyze` (self-check block placeholder: findings 0, clean true). Findings fixed during authoring: four RULE effects (041 on deactivation, 039 on upload / removal, 001 / 032 inside import rows) moved out of `Validations` because they raise no error at those endpoints.

| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-064 | scale code length fixed after creation; type and decimals only change how the embedded value is read | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-065 | CAT lookup keys served through OwnedLookups (API-CAT-052); amends ADR-CAT-050 | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-066 | in-process authorization raised as CORE-GAP; ADR-CAT-061 conditional | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-067 | every intra-module FK composite with TENANT_ID | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-068 | three repair operations stay allowed on an inactive Item (categoryFk-only update, Variant generation, Bundle components); amends ADR-CAT-063 | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-069 | reactivation of Categories, Variant attributes and values (API-CAT-053 … 055) | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-070 | Variant attributes and values stay CAT tables (checklist C5 / F3) | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/ADR-CAT-071 | item import time budget, batched code allocation, one scale-prefix lock per import | ACCEPTED (non-breaking) |
| decisions/CAT/CORE-GAP-in-process-authorization | how a cross-module read API is authorized for callers without a permission of the owning module | OPEN (asked of erp-core) |
| decisions/CAT/CORE-GAP-tenant-lookup-types | whether a tenant can create lookup types with nested values at runtime | OPEN (asked of erp-core) |
| decisions/CAT/CORE-GAP-number-series-block-allocation | whether NumberSeriesApi can allocate a block of n numbers in one call | OPEN (asked of erp-core) |

Next free ADR after the fourth gate-analysis revise (which raised none): ADR-CAT-072. None BLOCKED.

Fourth gate-analysis revise (backend): API-CAT-047's facade carries only `@PreAuthorize("isAuthenticated()")` and the read service checks PERM_CAT_ITEMS_VIEW in code (G1); API-CAT-049's write pass re-reads its Categories FOR SHARE after the scale-prefix lock and rejects rows of a Category deactivated meanwhile with RULE-CAT-026 (G2); API-CAT-041 / QR-CAT-026 apply `isActiveFl` to attributes and values alike (G3); API-CAT-049 traces REQ-CAT-139, the import time limit (G4); API-CAT-004 leaves an Item's barcodes active and API-CAT-016 stays allowed on an inactive Item (G6).

### Traceability
REQ covered by ≥ 1 API: 139 / 139 · orphan REQ: none · every API traces ≥ 1 REQ and ≥ 1 DBF · every PHASE / SUB / atom carries traces=.

### Event
"P3.1 completed: CAT v2 — API 55 · QR 72 · error codes 60 · XM 0 · ADR 5 (044–048) + gate-analysis revises 055–071, none BLOCKED · CORE-GAP 3"

        in: query

components:
  securitySchemes:
    bearerAuth: {type: http, scheme: bearer, bearerFormat: JWT}
  schemas:
    ItemSummary:
      type: object
      properties:
        itemPk: {type: integer, format: int64}
        code: {type: string}
        nameAr: {type: string}
        nameEn: {type: string}
        displayNameEn: {type: string}
        categoryFk: {type: integer, format: int64}
        brandCode: {type: string}
        itemKindCode: {type: string}
        publishStateCode: {type: string}
        isActiveFl: {type: boolean}
    ItemDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        itemPk: {type: integer, format: int64}
        code: {type: string}
        nameAr: {type: string}
        nameEn: {type: string}
        displayNameEn: {type: string}
        itemKindCode: {type: string}
        baseUomCode: {type: string}
        categoryFk: {type: integer, format: int64}
        brandCode: {type: string}
        taxClassCode: {type: string}
        einvoiceCodeTypeCode: {type: string}
        einvoiceCode: {type: string}
        descriptionAr: {type: string}
        descriptionEn: {type: string}
        publishStateCode: {type: string}
        isActiveFl: {type: boolean}
        version: {type: integer, format: int64}
        createdBy: {type: string}
        createdAt: {type: string, format: date-time}
        updatedBy: {type: string}
        updatedAt: {type: string, format: date-time}
        variants:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/VariantSummary'}
        itemUnits:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/ItemUnitView'}
        bundleComponents:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/BundleComponentView'}
        media:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/MediaView'}
    VariantSummary:
      type: object
      properties:
        variantPk: {type: integer, format: int64}
        code: {type: string}
        nameAr: {type: string}
        nameEn: {type: string}
        isDefaultFl: {type: boolean}
        isActiveFl: {type: boolean}
        einvoiceCodeTypeCode: {type: string}
        einvoiceCode: {type: string}
        effectiveEinvoiceCodeTypeCode: {type: string}
        effectiveEinvoiceCode: {type: string}
        version: {type: integer, format: int64}
        attributeValues:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AttributeValueView'}
        barcodes:
          type: array
          items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/BarcodeView'}
    VariantDetail:
      type: object
      properties:
        variantPk: {type: integer, format: int64}

P0 delta note: the given's module set changed (1.6.0 adds `gl`), so the domain-profile hand re-run named in the summary applies (OI-02).

P0.5 delta note: 22 user stories were added, covering 9 of 9 POL-CAT policies. No story is deferred. The erp-core 1.6.0 change (`gl`) does not touch the catalogue boundary, so no story depends on the given upgrade.

P1 delta note: the SRS is written in full, because the current state holds no earlier SRS. It adds REQ 127, AC 129, ENT 12, RULE 49 and SCR-REQ 6, covering 22 of 22 stories. It records ADR-CAT-038 … ADR-CAT-043 (all ACCEPTED, none BLOCKED). The `module-dependencies` block is `consumes: []`. The given uses go through erp-core mdl, cu, sequence, file, events, report and sec; no use goes through the new `gl` module.

P1.5 delta note: the contract is written in full, because the current state holds no earlier contract. It adds CON 18:
- 6 entity items, one for each SHARED ENT (ENT-CAT-001, 002, 006, 007, 008, 009), each with a BIGINT identifier.
- 11 operation items (signatures only).
- 1 catalogue-change event item.

Nothing is MODIFIED or REMOVED, so no consumer edge is affected. The contract repeats the SRS `given-usage` rows verbatim. No question was raised and no ADR was needed; the next free ADR is ADR-CAT-044.

P2 delta note: the db-script is written as a create-all migration, because the current state holds no earlier db-script. It adds 12 tables (one per ENT) and DBF 161 (001 … 161):
- every PK uses the identity clause, as a BIGINT, which matches the contract's identifier promise;
- 13 intra-module FKs;
- every UNIQUE is composite with TENANT_ID.

The XM register is empty (`consumes: []`), so `gov.py graph` gets no edge from CAT. The given tables (mdl lookup tables, CORE_NUMBER_SERIES, FILE_DOCUMENT, CORE_TENANT) are never re-created. The 10 CAT lookup keys (34 values), the 3 number series and the 8 Unit e-invoice codes are seeded for the PLATFORM tenant. Decisions are recorded as DEFAULTs D-P2-01 … D-P2-18. No question was raised and no ADR was needed; the next free ADR is unchanged.

P3.1 delta note: the backend execution plan is written in full, because the current state holds no earlier plan. It adds API 51 (001 … 051) and QR 70 (001 … 070) in the profile's seven phases; INT-C, the last phase, carries no block because the XM register is empty and no edge was minted. Every CON-CAT item (001 … 018) is honoured. The Error Catalog has 57 codes (49 RULE rows, 8 PLATFORM-STD). The API document `api-spec-cat.yaml` (OpenAPI 3.1.0) is derived from the plan with one operation per API block. ADR-CAT-044 … ADR-CAT-048 are recorded (all ACCEPTED, non-breaking, none BLOCKED); the next free ADR is ADR-CAT-049. No question was raised.

P3.2 delta note: the frontend design and the frontend execution plan are written in full, because the current state holds no earlier frontend artifact. The ui-ux-spec adds SCR 6 (001 … 006, one per SCR-REQ) and UXD 3 (001 … 003). The three UXDs are lookup values, capability flags and image files; all three are served by the given erp-core, and none of them is an XM. The flow diagram holds 18 flows covering 22 of 22 stories. The plan binds to `api-spec-cat.yaml` (the mock): 126 REQ → API bindings, 49 runtime codes → RULE, and 49 of 51 operations used. In the profile's six frontend phases it has 24 per-screen SUBs and one `screen-hooks` block per screen. ADR-CAT-049 … ADR-CAT-051 are recorded (all ACCEPTED, non-breaking, none BLOCKED); the next free ADR is ADR-CAT-052. No question was raised.

P4 delta note: both test plans are written in full, because the current state holds no earlier test plan. The TC stream starts at 001 and is shared by the two plans: TC-CAT-001 … 124 are backend and TC-CAT-125 … 182 are frontend.
- Coverage over the union of the two plans is AC 129 / 129, REQ 127 / 127, API 51 / 51, RULE 49 / 49, SCR 6 / 6 and UXD 3 / 3.
- The backend `INT-XM` phase is absent, because there is no XM. The frontend `INT-UXD` phase holds 6 TCs, for the three UXDs owned by the given erp-core.
- ADR-CAT-052 is recorded (ACCEPTED, non-breaking). It covers the track split, the package binding, the host-data creation surface, catalogue isolation, the fields `api-spec-cat.yaml` does not expose (next-action code, listed Variants, Variant `updatedAt`) and fault injection.
- Round 2 (after `gov.py analyze` refused C10.7 and C10.8): ADR-CAT-053 is recorded (ACCEPTED, non-breaking). Eight frontend TCs (TC-CAT-130, 143, 144, 145, 150, 157, 165, 171) now assert the routing and unchanged display of codes whose words the frontend plan does not bind; their wording stays asserted on the backend track. The unseeded AC values CAT_UOM `BAG` and CAT_EINVOICE_UNIT `H87`, for which no named add-value call exists, are replaced by seeded values in TC-CAT-063, 120, 121, 122 and 175 (TC-CAT-138 drops its `BAG` mention). Every TC id keeps its number and stays ADDED in this version. Coverage is unchanged.
- The next free ADR is ADR-CAT-054. No question was raised.

<<<KB: profiles/commerce/knowledge/raw-idea.md>>>
# Platform Brief — Retail & Wholesale Commerce SaaS on erp-core

**Status:** approved vision, input to `domain-profile` · **Owner:** Hesham Ezzat · **Date:** 2026-10-21
**Given:** `erp-core` (multi-tenant core, deployment mode `single|multi`, business modules tenant-agnostic) — consumed via `gov.py given add erp-core --version <current>`; nothing the core provides is rebuilt.
**Technical base (approved):** Spring Boot (Java 25) backend on `erp-core` · React frontend, one build for all deployments reading `GET /api/public/app-config` (tenancy mode, feature flags, activity preset) · PostgreSQL 16 (Flyway, app migrations `V1000+`). Jasper for printing via the core `PrintApi`.

---

## 1. One-paragraph vision

A SaaS platform for small and medium **retail and wholesale traders** in Egypt and the Gulf, covering back-office (purchasing, inventory, sales, receivables/payables, accounting, tax & e-invoicing), point of sale, and an **integrated e-commerce storefront** — one catalogue, one stock, one customer base. It serves many trading activities (electronics & appliances, apparel, household goods, cosmetics, grocery, spare parts, building materials, …) from **one codebase** through six activity capability flags and per-activity presets. It is **feature-complete but simple to use**: every capability exists, nothing is forced on the user; power lives in the backend, the UI shows only what is enabled.

## 2. Market and positioning (facts gathered 2026-09)

- Wholesale & retail trade is the largest activity by number of establishments: **54.4%** of Egypt's 3.9M establishments (6th Economic Census 2022/23) and **30.7%** of Saudi SMEs (Monsha'at SME Monitor).
- Demand is regulation-driven: **mandatory e-invoicing** (Saudi ZATCA phase-2 waves; Egypt B2C e-receipt expansion from Sept 2025) forces every small trader to adopt compliant software.
- Competitors in the segment are all SaaS (Qoyod, Wafeq, Daftra, …); the differentiator is **one product for back-office + POS + storefront**, activity-aware, and simple.

## 3. Fixed decisions (P0 — not open questions)

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| D1 | Activity scope: **retail & wholesale, multi-activity**, driven by six capability flags (§4). Pharmacies, restaurants, gold, vehicles, manufacturing, contracting, services are **out of scope** (different business models). |
| D2 | Business logic reads **capability flags only**, never the activity name. The activity is a **preset** that sets flags. Enforced by ArchUnit/lint. |
| D3 | Deployment: `erp.tenancy.mode=multi` (SaaS) is the default go-to-market; `single` available for dedicated/on-prem customers. Modules are tenant-agnostic (core rule). |
| D4 | Public storefront tenant resolution: **subdomain** (`<shop>.<platform>`) by default, **custom domain** optional; resolver feeds the core `TenantContext`; in `single` mode it returns the default tenant. Designed once in the Storefront API. |
| D5 | **Guest checkout allowed**; the guest becomes a CUSTOMER-realm account after the first order (core customer realm). |
| D6 | Approvals are a **lightweight business module** (`Approvals`): per document type → none / one step / role-based steps, configured on one screen. No BPM engine (core decision upheld). |
| D7 | Accounting is fully present (GL, cost centres, budgets) but invisible by default; auto-posting from documents; "accounting mode" reveals its screens. |
| D8 | Roles: three presets (Owner, Seller/Cashier, Warehouse) + custom roles via core permissions. |
| D9 | Simplicity is a measurable requirement (§6), not a feature cut. |
| D10 | Delivery is module-by-module in the fixed order of §5; a module is Done only with its reports, tests and conformance scenarios green. |

## 4. Capability flags and activity presets

### 4.1 The six flags (read through one `FeatureFlags` API)

| Flag | What it changes | Core seam used |
|---|---|---|
| `variants` | items have size/colour/… variants; catalogue, cart, stock at variant level | custom attributes |
| `serialTracking` (+ `warranty`) | serial captured on receipt/sale; warranty card; service returns | new business module `SerialWarranty` |
| `lotExpiry` | lot + expiry on receipt; FEFO issue; near-expiry reports | core `lot_id` + policy |
| `dualQuantity` | count + weight/length; pricing per either unit | core dual-quantity columns |
| `bundles` | composite items / kits / promo packs | — |
| `b2bCredit` | wholesale customers: credit limits, terms, price lists | Pricing + AR |

Each flag has tests in both states; the UI shows fields/menus/reports only when the flag is on.

### 4.2 Presets (✓ required · ○ optional)

| Activity | variants | serial | lotExpiry | dualQty | bundles | b2b |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Electronics & appliances, mobiles, computers | ○ | ✓ | | | ✓ | ✓ |
| Apparel & footwear | ✓ | | | | | ✓ |
| Household, cosmetics, stationery, toys, accessories | ○ | | | | ✓ | ✓ |
| Grocery & food | | | ✓ | ○ | | ✓ |
| Cleaning supplies, feed | | | ✓ | | | ✓ |
| Auto spare parts | | ○ | | | | ✓ |
| Power tools & hardware | | ✓ | | | ✓ | ✓ |
| Furniture & lighting | ✓ | | | | ✓ | |
| Sanitary & plumbing | ✓ | | | | | ✓ |
| Fabrics & furnishings | ✓ | | | ✓ (metre) | | ✓ |
| Building materials & steel | | | | ✓ | | ✓ |
| Produce / meat by weight | | | ✓ | ✓ | | |

Launch priority: electronics & appliances → apparel → household/cosmetics (zero or one extension each); grocery next (lot/expiry + delivery).

## 5. Module map (business modules the factory generates) — fixed DAG

| # | Module | Depends on | Core given used |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Catalog** — item, variants, media, categories, publish state | — | files (public), lookups, settings |
| 2 | **Pricing** — price lists, promotions, coupons, loyalty points | Catalog | settings |
| 3 | **Inventory** — stock, reservations, transfers, counts, lot/expiry, dual qty | Catalog | sequences, events, audit |
| 4 | **Party** — customers (incl. CUSTOMER-realm users), suppliers, addresses, credit | — | auth (realms), settings |
| 5 | **Purchasing** — PO, goods receipt, supplier invoice | 1,3,4 | sequences, print |
| 6 | **Sales & POS** — quotation, order, invoice, returns/exchange; POS mode (offline-capable) | 1,2,3,4 | sequences, print, events |
| 7 | **Settlements** — collections, payments, AR/AP ageing, payment gateway adapter | 4,5,6 | cash&bank seams, audit |
| 8 | **Tax & E-invoicing** — VAT, country adapters (ETA/ZATCA/Oman), tax returns | 5,6 | tax adapter |
| 9 | **Accounting** — auto-posting rules, GL, cost centres, budgets, period close | 5,6,7,8 | report/print |
| 10 | **SerialWarranty** — serials, warranty cards, service returns (flag `serialTracking`) | 3,6 | — |
| 11 | **Approvals** — per-document approval steps (D6) | any document module | events, notifications |
| 12 | **Shipping** — delivery status, carrier adapters | 6 | notifications |
| 13 | **Storefront** — public catalogue/search, guest cart, checkout, customer area; tenant-by-host resolver (D4) | 1,2,3,4,6,7,12 | auth realms, files public |
| 14 | **Insights** — dashboards, 15–20 standard reports, saved filters | all | report engine + Jasper |

Rules: dependencies flow downward only; cross-module calls only through declared `crossmodule` APIs; core only through manifest extension points (`C-GIVEN-1/2`).

## 6. Simplicity as a requirement ("everything available, nothing imposed")

1. **Progressive disclosure**: every screen starts minimal; "Advanced" reveals the rest; ≤ 4 mandatory fields per screen.
2. **Presets then customise**: choosing the activity fills all settings; later changes never require re-setup. Flags may be turned **on** at any time; turning off is blocked when data depends on it.
3. **Only enabled is shown**: menus, fields, reports, and storefront features bound to flags — no dead menus.
4. **Action in place**: return from the invoice, collect from the statement, receive from the PO.
5. **No dead ends**: every error offers a next action; undo for unposted operations.
6. **One global search** (items, customers, documents).
7. **Mobile-first** for selling, collecting, counting; responsive web, no separate app in v1.
8. **Defaults ("everything available, nothing imposed")**: one warehouse/branch/currency/price list to start; every such dimension exists in the data model from day one (e.g. every stock move carries `warehouse_id`); the UI hides the selector while the count is 1 and reveals it — plus the related actions (e.g. inter-warehouse transfer) and report filters — automatically when a second one is added. No data migration, no "mode" switch.
9. **Setup wizard (5 steps)**: company → activity preset → tax & e-invoice → payment methods → import items from Excel.
10. **Metric**: an owner with no technical background completes setup and issues the first invoice in **≤ 30 minutes** without training; a new employee sells after **≤ 10 minutes**.

## 7. Coverage and architecture guarantees (how we know it is sound)

| Risk | Guarantee | Enforced in |
|---|---|---|
| Activity "covered" on paper only | One **conformance scenario per preset** (setup → receive → sell → return → collect → report) runs in CI | `activity-conformance` test suite |
| Behaviour varies by activity ad hoc | Logic reads the six flags via `FeatureFlags` only; every flag tested in both states | ArchUnit + lint (`activity ==` or preset read outside settings = build failure) |
| Missing requirement for an activity | **Flag × activity matrix** is a P0 artifact; each cell traces to an EARS requirement ID (P1) and a `TC-*`; an untraced cell fails the consistency check | Factory traceability P0→P1→test-gen |
| Random inter-module design | Fixed DAG (§5); `crossmodule` contracts only; core via manifest extension points | ArchUnit boundaries + `C-GIVEN-1/2` + pass-review checklist (`erp-core-usage`) |
| Decisions drift during build | §3 decisions enter P0 as fixed + ADRs; no open questions after the PRD | One-gate analysis + adversarial reading |
| Uncontrolled growth | Fixed delivery order; a module is Done with reports, tests, conformance green | execution-state gates |

**Definition of "covers activity X":** preset exists + conformance scenario green + every matrix cell traced. Adding an activity later = one matrix column + one scenario.

## 8. Delivery order

1. Catalog, Party, Inventory, Purchasing, Sales & POS, Settlements, Tax & E-invoicing (sellable back-office + POS).
2. Storefront + Shipping (e-commerce live).
3. Accounting (visible mode), SerialWarranty, Approvals, Insights.
4. Advanced: multi-currency, cost centres/budgets, loyalty, carrier adapters, AI assistant (deferred core module).

## 9. Out of scope (v1)

Native mobile apps; marketplace (multi-vendor in one storefront); manufacturing/contracting/services modules; pharmacy/restaurant/gold/vehicle models; report designer; BPM workflow engine.

## 10. Next steps

1. Create project `commerce` from the governance-shared template; `gov.py given add erp-core --version <current>`.
2. Place this brief as the platform-brief input; run `domain-profile` → expect §4/§5 reproduced with core items marked `EXISTING (given)`.
3. P0 with §3 as fixed decisions and §4.2 as the matrix artifact → P1 (EARS + traceability) → … → packages → build in §8 order with the `erp-core-usage` skill.
<<<END KB>>>
